| [`class RenderingDevice`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/RenderingDevice.xml) | [`public sealed class Electron2D.RenderingDevice`](../../classes/RenderingDevice.md) | Partial | ADR 0028: exact queried MSAA render targets, copied color/depth/resolve slots, native alpha-to-coverage, automatic store-and-resolve and standalone full color resolves with preserved source samples. GLSL/HLSL native fractional edges, depth, reordered MRT resolve, retained canvas and prepared zero owner-thread managed bytes are verified on Linux/Vulkan; native allocations and foreign platforms remain unverified. Native views/transient last-use, raw depth/sample transfer and shader/dynamic-state adapters remain independently accounted for. ADR 0028: executable copied push bytes for compute/vertex/fragment on the shared device timeline. Original 32-bit and FP64 scalar/vector, compact array, row/column-major matrix and nested-struct layouts lower to a hidden std140 raw-word uniform block; public sets retain their logical bindings. Exact reflected byte count, portable 128-byte bound, per-pipeline/list initialization and recorded snapshots execute. GLSL/HLSL native results, 128-byte/three-uniform capacity, retained canvas and 32 warmed owner-thread zero-managed-byte cycles per compute/graphics language pass on Linux/Vulkan; foreign targets and native allocations remain unverified. ADR 0028: typed copied Boolean/SByte/Byte/Int16/UInt16/Int32/UInt32/Int64/UInt64/Half/Single/Double specialization values freeze scalar defaults and dependent integer/logical/vector/composite expressions before native pipeline creation. Compute workgroup dimensions and uniform/push array layouts use specialized values. Shader identities remain borrowed; native variants are owned and released by pipelines. Unassigned descriptions, unknown/duplicate/negative identifiers, scalar type mismatch, malformed/cyclic/undefined expressions and exceeding native limits reject. Linux/Vulkan compute values, paired graphics pixels, retained canvas and 32 warmed zero-owner-thread-managed-byte cycles execute; foreign targets and native allocations remain unverified. Ordered framebuffer passes, readonly single/multisample input attachments, preservation and per-pass pipeline selection now execute with native pixels and prepared zero-managed-owner-byte checks; see the member rows for remaining boundaries. Native FP64 arithmetic, raw SSBO/UBO and scalar/vector/matrix/array/struct push layouts now execute with enabled Vulkan shaderFloat64; typed Double values preserve two-word defaults/overrides, floating conversions and composite selection. Optional refusal preserves ordinary device creation. DeviceFP64.Consumer verifies GLSL/HLSL native compute and graphics; direct HLSL double specialization remains blocked by pinned DXC, while float-to-double specialization executes. Narrow-integer precision, 64-bit image atomics, 64-bit vertex/texel formats and pointer capabilities retain their separate adapters. Native Int64 now executes signed/unsigned arithmetic, raw buffers/push data, typed long/ulong specialization and bounded wide array/index lowering. Independent Vulkan shaderInt64/FP64 refusal preserves the remaining profile. Wide interstage values match complete location footprints. DeviceInt64.Consumer covers native GLSL/HLSL outputs and the mixed unsigned-to-FP64 path; narrow precision, 64-bit image atomics and vertex/texel formats retain separate adapters. Native Half arithmetic and packed SSBO/UBO/push execute through independently enabled Vulkan 1.2 features. DeviceFP16.Consumer covers GLSL/HLSL compute/graphics/canvas, copied typed values, compact layouts, raw bits and feature refusal. Direct HLSL Half specialization requires frontend support; storage-only Half push requires the Float16 reconstruction adapter, and native 16-bit stage IO requires its feature. See docs/components/local-compute.md#native-half-arithmetic-and-packed-data. Native Int16/UInt16 arithmetic and packed SSBO/UBO/push data execute with independent shaderInt16/storage opt-in. Typed short/ushort values preserve exact bits, signed literal extension and width-correct operations/conversions; narrow array extents normalize before layout. DeviceInt16.Consumer covers GLSL/HLSL compute/graphics, retained canvas, zero warmed owner bytes and independent integer/Half/storage refusal. Exact integer push also requires Int16 arithmetic. Stage16 IO, non-32-bit vertex/color formats, native allocations and foreign devices remain separate; see local-compute native Int16 section. Native Int8/UInt8 arithmetic, one-byte SSBO/UBO/push and typed sbyte/byte overrides execute with independent Vulkan 1.2 features. Mixed byte/Int16/Half layouts retain exact bits/alignment; direct byte extents and dependent conversions resolve before native creation. DeviceInt8.Consumer covers source/bytecode compute, actual graphics/canvas, zero warmed owner bytes and byte/16-bit sibling refusal. Actual byte GLSL uses modern storage classes; HLSL native8 syntax requires its frontend upgrade. Byte stage IO, native formats and foreign/native-allocation acceptance remain separate; see local-compute byte section. ADR 0028: ordinary writable vertex/fragment StorageBuffer declarations now execute through independently enabled native stage features and external draw/pass memory dependencies, including shared bindings, GPU-produced indirect batches, multiview/subpasses and following compute/readback. Unsupported stages reject before identity/binary publication. See docs/components/local-compute.md#writable-graphics-storage-buffers; ordinary graphics Image stores and R32 atomics now execute through mixed native descriptors, original-image GENERAL scopes and independently enabled writing stages; 64-bit graphics atomics retain their adapter. See docs/components/graphics-storage-images.md. ADR 0028: writable vertex/fragment ImageBuffer stores and R32 atomics now execute through existing encoded buffers and draw dependencies, including packed neighbors, descriptor choices/specialized counts and later sampled/compute/CPU consumers; native texel-only capabilities are removed and core graphics no longer forces Vulkan 1.2. HLSL source specialization-array lengths retain their pinned frontend dependency; see docs/components/local-compute.md#writable-formatted-graphics-producers. |

| [`enum_value TEXTURE_SLICE_2D [TextureSliceType] = 0`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/RenderingDevice.xml) | [`public const Electron2D.RenderingDevice.TextureSliceType Texture = 0`](../../classes/RenderingDevice.TextureSliceType.md) | Implemented | Numeric identity matches the compiled C# declaration. ADR 0028: native original-owner format/layer/mip views execute initial overrides, sampled maps, per-layer image stores/R32 atomics, color/depth/stencil targets, MSAA resolve, readonly input snapshots, raw transfers and retained canvas. Stored GetFormat metadata remains distinct from effective shader/attachment encoding. Nonempty shareable lists include the exact base format and validate native compatibility/usage/sample roles. Owner retirement automatically frees views; intermediate views preserve siblings. Native refusal rolls back pins/images/memory. See docs/components/shared-texture-views.md for executable checks and platform/native-heap limits. |

| [`enum_value UNIFORM_TYPE_IMAGE [UniformType] = 3`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/RenderingDevice.xml) | [`public const Electron2D.RenderingDevice.UniformType Image = 3`](../../classes/RenderingDevice.UniformType.md) | Implemented | ADR 0028: executable owner-thread local compute, device-owned RIDs, copied SPIR-V buffer interfaces, descriptor remapping, ordered dispatch/barriers, explicit submit/sync/readback and validated lifetime. RenderingDeviceTests exercises real native computation and errors on Linux/Vulkan; other platforms remain unverified. ADR 0028: ordinary writable vertex/fragment StorageBuffer declarations now execute through independently enabled native stage features and external draw/pass memory dependencies, including shared bindings, GPU-produced indirect batches, multiview/subpasses and following compute/readback. Unsupported stages reject before identity/binary publication. See docs/components/local-compute.md#writable-graphics-storage-buffers; ordinary graphics Image stores and R32 atomics now execute through mixed native descriptors, original-image GENERAL scopes and independently enabled writing stages; 64-bit graphics atomics retain their adapter. See docs/components/graphics-storage-images.md. ADR 0028: writable vertex/fragment ImageBuffer stores and R32 atomics now execute through existing encoded buffers and draw dependencies, including packed neighbors, descriptor choices/specialized counts and later sampled/compute/CPU consumers; native texel-only capabilities are removed and core graphics no longer forces Vulkan 1.2. HLSL source specialization-array lengths retain their pinned frontend dependency; see docs/components/local-compute.md#writable-formatted-graphics-producers. |

TextureBuffer, SamplerWithTextureBuffer and ImageBuffer execute typed fetch/read/query, compute stores and signed/unsigned R32 atomics; graphics-to-retained-canvas consumers and prepared zero-owner-managed-byte intervals execute in GLSL/HLSL on software Linux. Native format/precision/stage/capacity limits remain explicit; hardware, native allocator and foreign platforms remain unverified. ADR 0028: writable vertex/fragment ImageBuffer stores and R32 atomics now execute through existing encoded buffers and draw dependencies, including packed neighbors, descriptor choices/specialized counts and later sampled/compute/CPU consumers; native texel-only capabilities are removed and core graphics no longer forces Vulkan 1.2. HLSL source specialization-array lengths retain their pinned frontend dependency; see docs/components/local-compute.md#writable-formatted-graphics-producers. |

| [`method compute_list_set_push_constant(int compute_list, PackedByteArray buffer, int size_bytes) -> void`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/RenderingDevice.xml) | [`public System.Void ComputeListSetPushConstant(System.Int64 computeList, System.ReadOnlySpan<System.Byte> buffer, System.UInt32 sizeBytes)`](../../classes/RenderingDevice.md) | Implemented | ADR 0028: executable copied push bytes for compute/vertex/fragment on the shared device timeline. Original 32-bit and FP64 scalar/vector, compact array, row/column-major matrix and nested-struct layouts lower to a hidden std140 raw-word uniform block; public sets retain their logical bindings. Exact reflected byte count, portable 128-byte bound, per-pipeline/list initialization and recorded snapshots execute. GLSL/HLSL native results, 128-byte/three-uniform capacity, retained canvas and 32 warmed owner-thread zero-managed-byte cycles per compute/graphics language pass on Linux/Vulkan; foreign targets and native allocations remain unverified. Native FP64 arithmetic, raw SSBO/UBO and scalar/vector/matrix/array/struct push layouts now execute with enabled Vulkan shaderFloat64; typed Double values preserve two-word defaults/overrides, floating conversions and composite selection. Optional refusal preserves ordinary device creation. DeviceFP64.Consumer verifies GLSL/HLSL native compute and graphics; direct HLSL double specialization remains blocked by pinned DXC, while float-to-double specialization executes. Narrow-integer precision, 64-bit image atomics, 64-bit vertex/texel formats and pointer capabilities retain their separate adapters. Native Int64 now executes signed/unsigned arithmetic, raw buffers/push data, typed long/ulong specialization and bounded wide array/index lowering. Independent Vulkan shaderInt64/FP64 refusal preserves the remaining profile. Wide interstage values match complete location footprints. DeviceInt64.Consumer covers native GLSL/HLSL outputs and the mixed unsigned-to-FP64 path; narrow precision, 64-bit image atomics and vertex/texel formats retain separate adapters. Native Half arithmetic and packed SSBO/UBO/push execute through independently enabled Vulkan 1.2 features. DeviceFP16.Consumer covers GLSL/HLSL compute/graphics/canvas, copied typed values, compact layouts, raw bits and feature refusal. Direct HLSL Half specialization requires frontend support; storage-only Half push requires the Float16 reconstruction adapter, and native 16-bit stage IO requires its feature. See docs/components/local-compute.md#native-half-arithmetic-and-packed-data. Native Int16/UInt16 arithmetic and packed SSBO/UBO/push data execute with independent shaderInt16/storage opt-in. Typed short/ushort values preserve exact bits, signed literal extension and width-correct operations/conversions; narrow array extents normalize before layout. DeviceInt16.Consumer covers GLSL/HLSL compute/graphics, retained canvas, zero warmed owner bytes and independent integer/Half/storage refusal. Exact integer push also requires Int16 arithmetic. Stage16 IO, non-32-bit vertex/color formats, native allocations and foreign devices remain separate; see local-compute native Int16 section. Native Int8/UInt8 arithmetic, one-byte SSBO/UBO/push and typed sbyte/byte overrides execute with independent Vulkan 1.2 features. Mixed byte/Int16/Half layouts retain exact bits/alignment; direct byte extents and dependent conversions resolve before native creation. DeviceInt8.Consumer covers source/bytecode compute, actual graphics/canvas, zero warmed owner bytes and byte/16-bit sibling refusal. Actual byte GLSL uses modern storage classes; HLSL native8 syntax requires its frontend upgrade. Byte stage IO, native formats and foreign/native-allocation acceptance remain separate; see local-compute byte section. |
| [`method compute_pipeline_create(RID shader, RDPipelineSpecializationConstant[] specialization_constants = []) -> RID`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/RenderingDevice.xml) | [`public Electron2D.RID ComputePipelineCreate(Electron2D.RID shader, System.ReadOnlySpan<Electron2D.RDPipelineSpecializationConstant> specializationConstants = default)`](../../classes/RenderingDevice.md) | Implemented | ADR 0028: typed copied Boolean/SByte/Byte/Int16/UInt16/Int32/UInt32/Int64/UInt64/Half/Single/Double specialization values freeze scalar defaults and dependent integer/logical/vector/composite expressions before native pipeline creation. Compute workgroup dimensions and uniform/push array layouts use specialized values. Shader identities remain borrowed; native variants are owned and released by pipelines. Unassigned descriptions, unknown/duplicate/negative identifiers, scalar type mismatch, malformed/cyclic/undefined expressions and exceeding native limits reject. Linux/Vulkan compute values, paired graphics pixels, retained canvas and 32 warmed zero-owner-thread-managed-byte cycles execute; foreign targets and native allocations remain unverified. Native FP64 arithmetic, raw SSBO/UBO and scalar/vector/matrix/array/struct push layouts now execute with enabled Vulkan shaderFloat64; typed Double values preserve two-word defaults/overrides, floating conversions and composite selection. Optional refusal preserves ordinary device creation. DeviceFP64.Consumer verifies GLSL/HLSL native compute and graphics; direct HLSL double specialization remains blocked by pinned DXC, while float-to-double specialization executes. Narrow-integer precision, 64-bit image atomics, 64-bit vertex/texel formats and pointer capabilities retain their separate adapters. Native Int64 now executes signed/unsigned arithmetic, raw buffers/push data, typed long/ulong specialization and bounded wide array/index lowering. Independent Vulkan shaderInt64/FP64 refusal preserves the remaining profile. Wide interstage values match complete location footprints. DeviceInt64.Consumer covers native GLSL/HLSL outputs and the mixed unsigned-to-FP64 path; narrow precision, 64-bit image atomics and vertex/texel formats retain separate adapters. Native 64-bit compute buffer/workgroup atomics execute through independent Vulkan 1.2 features. DeviceAtomic64.Consumer verifies exact concurrent returns, CAS, rollover, copied values and storage-specific refusal; image/graphics atomic adapters remain separate. See the native atomic contract in docs/components/local-compute.md. Native Half arithmetic and packed SSBO/UBO/push execute through independently enabled Vulkan 1.2 features. DeviceFP16.Consumer covers GLSL/HLSL compute/graphics/canvas, copied typed values, compact layouts, raw bits and feature refusal. Direct HLSL Half specialization requires frontend support; storage-only Half push requires the Float16 reconstruction adapter, and native 16-bit stage IO requires its feature. See docs/components/local-compute.md#native-half-arithmetic-and-packed-data. Native Int16/UInt16 arithmetic and packed SSBO/UBO/push data execute with independent shaderInt16/storage opt-in. Typed short/ushort values preserve exact bits, signed literal extension and width-correct operations/conversions; narrow array extents normalize before layout. DeviceInt16.Consumer covers GLSL/HLSL compute/graphics, retained canvas, zero warmed owner bytes and independent integer/Half/storage refusal. Exact integer push also requires Int16 arithmetic. Stage16 IO, non-32-bit vertex/color formats, native allocations and foreign devices remain separate; see local-compute native Int16 section. Native Int8/UInt8 arithmetic, one-byte SSBO/UBO/push and typed sbyte/byte overrides execute with independent Vulkan 1.2 features. Mixed byte/Int16/Half layouts retain exact bits/alignment; direct byte extents and dependent conversions resolve before native creation. DeviceInt8.Consumer covers source/bytecode compute, actual graphics/canvas, zero warmed owner bytes and byte/16-bit sibling refusal. Actual byte GLSL uses modern storage classes; HLSL native8 syntax requires its frontend upgrade. Byte stage IO, native formats and foreign/native-allocation acceptance remain separate; see local-compute byte section. |

Actual byte GLSL uses modern storage classes; HLSL native8 syntax requires its frontend upgrade. Byte stage IO, native formats and foreign/native-allocation acceptance remain separate; see local-compute byte section. |

Unassigned descriptions, unknown/duplicate/negative identifiers, scalar type mismatch, malformed/cyclic/undefined expressions and exceeding native limits reject. Nonzero render-pass selection now executes against the copied target pass; copied per-face/depth-bounds state now executes through the native adapter; additional dynamic states retain their separate dependencies. Optional refusal preserves ordinary device creation. DeviceFP64.Consumer verifies GLSL/HLSL native compute and graphics; direct HLSL double specialization remains blocked by pinned DXC, while float-to-double specialization executes. Narrow-integer precision, 64-bit image atomics, 64-bit vertex/texel formats and pointer capabilities retain their separate adapters. Native Int64 now executes signed/unsigned arithmetic, raw buffers/push data, typed long/ulong specialization and bounded wide array/index lowering. Independent Vulkan shaderInt64/FP64 refusal preserves the remaining profile. Wide interstage values match complete location footprints. DeviceInt64.Consumer covers native GLSL/HLSL outputs and the mixed unsigned-to-FP64 path; narrow precision, 64-bit image atomics and vertex/texel formats retain separate adapters. Native Half arithmetic and packed SSBO/UBO/push execute through independently enabled Vulkan 1.2 features. DeviceFP16.Consumer covers GLSL/HLSL compute/graphics/canvas, copied typed values, compact layouts, raw bits and feature refusal. Direct HLSL Half specialization requires frontend support; storage-only Half push requires the Float16 reconstruction adapter, and native 16-bit stage IO requires its feature. See docs/components/local-compute.md#native-half-arithmetic-and-packed-data. Native Int16/UInt16 arithmetic and packed SSBO/UBO/push data execute with independent shaderInt16/storage opt-in. Typed short/ushort values preserve exact bits, signed literal extension and width-correct operations/conversions; narrow array extents normalize before layout. DeviceInt16.Consumer covers GLSL/HLSL compute/graphics, retained canvas, zero warmed owner bytes and independent integer/Half/storage refusal. Exact integer push also requires Int16 arithmetic. Stage16 IO, non-32-bit vertex/color formats, native allocations and foreign devices remain separate; see local-compute native Int16 section. Native Int8/UInt8 arithmetic, one-byte SSBO/UBO/push and typed sbyte/byte overrides execute with independent Vulkan 1.2 features. Mixed byte/Int16/Half layouts retain exact bits/alignment; direct byte extents and dependent conversions resolve before native creation. DeviceInt8.Consumer covers source/bytecode compute, actual graphics/canvas, zero warmed owner bytes and byte/16-bit sibling refusal. Actual byte GLSL uses modern storage classes; HLSL native8 syntax requires its frontend upgrade. Byte stage IO, native formats and foreign/native-allocation acceptance remain separate; see local-compute byte section. ADR 0028: ordinary writable graphics StorageBuffer execution and inter-draw ordering now follow docs/components/local-compute.md#writable-graphics-storage-buffers; unsupported native stages reject before shader/binary publication. ADR 0028: writable vertex/fragment ImageBuffer stores and R32 atomics now execute through existing encoded buffers and draw dependencies, including packed neighbors, descriptor choices/specialized counts and later sampled/compute/CPU consumers; native texel-only capabilities are removed and core graphics no longer forces Vulkan 1.2. HLSL source specialization-array lengths retain their pinned frontend dependency; see docs/components/local-compute.md#writable-formatted-graphics-producers. Vertex-only/no-position programs and copied DiscardPrimitives now execute, including source, version-two binary round-trip, multiview and sampled variants; see docs/components/raster-discard.md. Broader row limits remain unchanged. |

ADR 0028: writable vertex/fragment ImageBuffer stores and R32 atomics now execute through existing encoded buffers and draw dependencies, including packed neighbors, descriptor choices/specialized counts and later sampled/compute/CPU consumers; native texel-only capabilities are removed and core graphics no longer forces Vulkan 1.2. HLSL source specialization-array lengths retain their pinned frontend dependency; see docs/components/local-compute.md#writable-formatted-graphics-producers. |
